Results · Definitions

← suite simplipy

sp-acj4-442-cond true

category simplipy-conditional

Summary

Given
\(x \in \mathbb{C},\quad x \in i\mathbb{R},\quad x \neq 0,\quad \log x \neq 0,\quad \frac{1}{\log x} \neq 0\)
Claim
\(\displaystyle -\log\left(\log x\right) = \log\left(\frac{1}{\log x}\right)\)
Expected
true equal at every admissible point

the two sides are equal at every admissible point

SimpliPy acj-4 (MIT) rules_real.json[442]: neg log log ?0 -> log inv log ?0 [weakest assumption found: imaginary]

Lean proof: included

Source (conditional.sexp)

(case sp-acj4-442-cond (category simplipy-conditional)
  (note "SimpliPy acj-4 (MIT) rules_real.json[442]: neg log log ?0  ->  log inv log ?0  [weakest assumption found: imaginary]")
  (var x complex)
  (assume (imaginary x))
  (assume (nonzero x))
  (assume (nonzero (log x)))
  (assume (nonzero (/ (log x))))
  (ask (= (- (log (log x))) (log (/ (log x)))))
  (expect true)
  (lean-proof "obtain ⟨y, hy, rfl⟩ := AE.cond_imaginary_exists h0.2; have hi : (AE.log (↑y * Complex.I)).im ≠ 0 := (by rw [AE.log_def, Complex.log_im, Ne, Complex.arg_eq_zero_iff]; simp [hy]); refine ⟨⟨⟨h1.2, h2.2⟩, ⟨h1.2, h2.2⟩, h3.2⟩, ?_⟩; rw [AE.cond_log_inv_of (Or.inr hi)]"))

Lean proofs (1; checked by lean-check, each under the statement it proves)

the claim
x ∈ ℂ
h0imaginary(x)
h1nonzero(x)
h2nonzero(log(x))
h3nonzero(1 / log(x))
⊢−log(log(x)) = log(1 / log(x))
definedness side conditions (7, and 3 trivially true; t↓: t is defined)
h0imaginary(x)↓
h1nonzero(x)↓ trivially true
h2x ≠ 0
h2nonzero(log(x))↓ trivially true
h3x ≠ 0
h3log(x) ≠ 0
h3nonzero(1 / log(x))↓ trivially true
⊢x ≠ 0 ×2
⊢log(x) ≠ 0 ×2
⊢1 / log(x) ≠ 0
Lean statement
theorem case_sp_acj4_442_cond (x : ℂ) (h0 : ((AE.imaginary.D x) ∧ (AE.imaginary x))) (h1 : ((AE.nonzero.D x) ∧ (AE.nonzero x))) (h2 : ((x ≠ 0 ∧ AE.nonzero.D (AE.log x)) ∧ (AE.nonzero (AE.log x)))) (h3 : (((x ≠ 0 ∧ AE.log x ≠ 0) ∧ AE.nonzero.D (AE.inv (AE.log x))) ∧ (AE.nonzero (AE.inv (AE.log x))))) : (((x ≠ 0 ∧ AE.log x ≠ 0) ∧ (x ≠ 0 ∧ AE.log x ≠ 0) ∧ AE.inv (AE.log x) ≠ 0) ∧ (-(AE.log (AE.log x)) = AE.log (AE.inv (AE.log x)))) :=
obtain ⟨y, hy, rfl⟩ := AE.cond_imaginary_exists h0.2; have hi : (AE.log (↑y * Complex.I)).im ≠ 0 := (by rw [AE.log_def, Complex.log_im, Ne, Complex.arg_eq_zero_iff]; simp [hy]); refine ⟨⟨⟨h1.2, h2.2⟩, ⟨h1.2, h2.2⟩, h3.2⟩, ?_⟩; rw [AE.cond_log_inv_of (Or.inr hi)]

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satassume.sympy_api
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satassume')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satassume.sympy_api.ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W), strict=True)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(x), ~Q.zero(log(x)), ~Q.zero(1 / log(x)))
Residual = -log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))
Answer = satassume.sympy_api.ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum, strict=True)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satsimp
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satsimp')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satsimp.refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(x), ~Q.zero(log(x)), ~Q.zero(1 / log(x)))
Residual = -log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))
Result = satsimp.refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satsimp
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satsimp')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satsimp.trigsimp(sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, Q.real(W), strict=True)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(x), ~Q.zero(log(x)), ~Q.zero(1 / log(x)))
Residual = -log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))
Result = satsimp.trigsimp(Residual, Assum, strict=True)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satsimp
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satsimp')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = simplify(satsimp.refine(sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, Q.positive(W), strict=True))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

Residual = -log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))
Result = simplify(satsimp.refine(Residual, Assum, strict=True))
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = (sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(W**2 - 2*W + 1 >= 0, W > 0), sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(Eq(log(W**2) - 2*log(W), 0), W > 0))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(x), ~Q.zero(log(x)), ~Q.zero(1 / log(x)))
Residual = -log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))
# the claim as a relation, sympy-extras' statement form (not Q.zero: see the adapter)
Answer = sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(Eq(Residual, 0), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')
difference-log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))→-log(1/log(x)) - log(log(x))
sympy_extras.assumptions.askEq(Residual, 0)→None

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
(
  $HistoryLength = 0;
  $Messages = {};
  Warmup = FullSimplify[Sin[W]^2 + Cos[W]^2, Element[W, Reals]];
  GenericWarmup = Quiet[{Simplify[Abs[W] + W, W < 0], Simplify[Sqrt[W^2] - W, W > 0], Refine[Sqrt[W^2], W > 0], Simplify[Log[W V] - Log[W] - Log[V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[Log[-W] - Log[W] - I Pi, W < 0], Simplify[(-1)^(2 W) - 1, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Mod[W + 1, 2], Element[W, Integers] && Element[(W - 1)/2, Integers]], Simplify[ArcTan[W] + ArcTan[1/W] - Pi/2, W > 0], FullSimplify[ArcCot[W] - ArcTan[1/W], W > 0], Simplify[Abs[Exp[I W]] - 1, Element[W, Reals]], Simplify[Re[W] - (W + Conjugate[W])/2], Simplify[Gamma[W + 1] - W!, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0], Simplify[Floor[W] - W, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Sqrt[W] Sqrt[V] - Sqrt[W V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[HeavisideTheta[W] - 1, W > 0], Simplify[2^W 2^V - 2^(W + V), Element[W, Reals] && Element[V, Reals]], Simplify[Sum[V, {V, 1, W}] - W (W + 1)/2, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0]}];
  Quiet[Check[Message[General::argx, "W", 0], Null]];
  {$Version, Warmup, Length[GenericWarmup]}
)
(* evaluated in a forked copy of the setup kernel: TimeConstrained would not fire here (no timer thread), so the parent enforces a hard timeout of 9 s by killing this process *)
(
  Assum = (Element[x, Complexes]) && ((Re[x] == 0 && x != 0)) && ((x != 0)) && ((Log[x] != 0)) && (((1/Log[x]) != 0));
  Residual = (-Log[Log[x]]) - (Log[1/Log[x]]);
  Result = FullSimplify[Residual, Assum];
  Answer = Which[Result === 0, True, NumericQ[Result] && TrueQ[Result != 0], False, True, Null];
  {Residual, Result, Answer}
)
difference(-Log[Log[x]]) - (Log[1/Log[x]])→-Log[Log[x]^(-1)] - Log[Log[x]]

Machines: pi5 (indicative): it has some other load: times are a fair guide, large differences between its runs mean something, small ones may be noise (Raspberry Pi 5, 4 cores. Benchmark runs go one at a time, in a low-priority systemd unit (CPUWeight=20, Nice=10); since 2026-10-17, each eval is pinned to core 3 (earlier runs were not pinned). The Pi also serves websites, and other work may share core 3 unless it is reserved for the runs (pi5-deploy INSTALL.md, step 15). Clock frequency and temperature are not controlled).

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satsimp
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satsimp')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satsimp.trigsimp(sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, Q.real(W), strict=True)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(x), ~Q.zero(log(x)), ~Q.zero(1 / log(x)))
Residual = -log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))
Result = satsimp.trigsimp(Residual, Assum, strict=True)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
from sympy.assumptions.satask import satask
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(x), ~Q.zero(log(x)), ~Q.zero(1 / log(x)))
Residual = -log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = satask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

sataskQ.zero(Residual)→None

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(x), ~Q.zero(log(x)), ~Q.zero(1 / log(x)))
Residual = -log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata

# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None

W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = (sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(W**2 - 2*W + 1 >= 0, W > 0), sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(Eq(log(W**2) - 2*log(W), 0), W > 0))

# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satsimp
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satsimp')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satsimp.refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(x), ~Q.zero(log(x)), ~Q.zero(1 / log(x)))
Residual = -log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))
Result = satsimp.refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(x), ~Q.zero(log(x)), ~Q.zero(1 / log(x)))
Residual = -log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sqrt(8) + sin(-W) + exp(log(W)) - Abs(-W)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True, imaginary=True, zero=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): ~Q.zero(log(x))
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): ~Q.zero(1 / log(x))
Residual = -log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))
# automatic evaluation only: no simplify, refine or other function
Result = Residual
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
from sympy.assumptions.satask import satask
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(x), ~Q.zero(log(x)), ~Q.zero(1 / log(x)))
Residual = -log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = satask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(x), ~Q.zero(log(x)), ~Q.zero(1 / log(x)))
Residual = -log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex

Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
(
  $HistoryLength = 0;
  $Messages = {};
  Warmup = FullSimplify[Sin[W]^2 + Cos[W]^2, Element[W, Reals]];
  GenericWarmup = Quiet[{Simplify[Abs[W] + W, W < 0], Simplify[Sqrt[W^2] - W, W > 0], Refine[Sqrt[W^2], W > 0], Simplify[Log[W V] - Log[W] - Log[V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[Log[-W] - Log[W] - I Pi, W < 0], Simplify[(-1)^(2 W) - 1, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Mod[W + 1, 2], Element[W, Integers] && Element[(W - 1)/2, Integers]], Simplify[ArcTan[W] + ArcTan[1/W] - Pi/2, W > 0], FullSimplify[ArcCot[W] - ArcTan[1/W], W > 0], Simplify[Abs[Exp[I W]] - 1, Element[W, Reals]], Simplify[Re[W] - (W + Conjugate[W])/2], Simplify[Gamma[W + 1] - W!, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0], Simplify[Floor[W] - W, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Sqrt[W] Sqrt[V] - Sqrt[W V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[HeavisideTheta[W] - 1, W > 0], Simplify[2^W 2^V - 2^(W + V), Element[W, Reals] && Element[V, Reals]], Simplify[Sum[V, {V, 1, W}] - W (W + 1)/2, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0]}];
  Quiet[Check[Message[General::argx, "W", 0], Null]];
  {$Version, Warmup, Length[GenericWarmup]}
)
(* evaluated in a forked copy of the setup kernel: TimeConstrained would not fire here (no timer thread), so the parent enforces a hard timeout of 9 s by killing this process *)
(
  Assum = (Element[x, Complexes]) && ((Re[x] == 0 && x != 0)) && ((x != 0)) && ((Log[x] != 0)) && (((1/Log[x]) != 0));
  Residual = (-Log[Log[x]]) - (Log[1/Log[x]]);
  Result = FullSimplify[Residual, Assum];
  Answer = Which[Result === 0, True, NumericQ[Result] && TrueQ[Result != 0], False, True, Null];

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satsimp
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satsimp')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satsimp.refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(x), ~Q.zero(log(x)), ~Q.zero(1 / log(x)))
Residual = -log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))
Result = satsimp.refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(x), ~Q.zero(log(x)), ~Q.zero(1 / log(x)))
Residual = -log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(x), ~Q.zero(log(x)), ~Q.zero(1 / log(x)))
Residual = -log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')
difference-log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))→-log(1/log(x)) - log(log(x))
sympy_extras.assumptions.simplifysympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)→-log(1/log(x)) - log(log(x))
answernull

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = (sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(W**2 - 2*W + 1 >= 0, W > 0), sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(Eq(log(W**2) - 2*log(W), 0), W > 0))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(x), ~Q.zero(log(x)), ~Q.zero(1 / log(x)))
Residual = -log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))
# the claim as a relation, sympy-extras' statement form (not Q.zero: see the adapter)
Answer = sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(Eq(Residual, 0), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

(
  $HistoryLength = 0;
  $Messages = {};
  Warmup = FullSimplify[Sin[W]^2 + Cos[W]^2, Element[W, Reals]];
  GenericWarmup = Quiet[{Simplify[Abs[W] + W, W < 0], Simplify[Sqrt[W^2] - W, W > 0], Refine[Sqrt[W^2], W > 0], Simplify[Log[W V] - Log[W] - Log[V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[Log[-W] - Log[W] - I Pi, W < 0], Simplify[(-1)^(2 W) - 1, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Mod[W + 1, 2], Element[W, Integers] && Element[(W - 1)/2, Integers]], Simplify[ArcTan[W] + ArcTan[1/W] - Pi/2, W > 0], FullSimplify[ArcCot[W] - ArcTan[1/W], W > 0], Simplify[Abs[Exp[I W]] - 1, Element[W, Reals]], Simplify[Re[W] - (W + Conjugate[W])/2], Simplify[Gamma[W + 1] - W!, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0], Simplify[Floor[W] - W, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Sqrt[W] Sqrt[V] - Sqrt[W V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[HeavisideTheta[W] - 1, W > 0], Simplify[2^W 2^V - 2^(W + V), Element[W, Reals] && Element[V, Reals]], Simplify[Sum[V, {V, 1, W}] - W (W + 1)/2, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0]}];
  Quiet[Check[Message[General::argx, "W", 0], Null]];
  {$Version, Warmup, Length[GenericWarmup]}
)

  Assum = (Element[x, Complexes]) && ((Re[x] == 0 && x != 0)) && ((x != 0)) && ((Log[x] != 0)) && (((1/Log[x]) != 0));
  Residual = (-Log[Log[x]]) - (Log[1/Log[x]]);
  Result = FullSimplify[Residual, Assum];
  Answer = Which[Result === 0, True, NumericQ[Result] && TrueQ[Result != 0], False, True, Null];
  {Residual, Result, Answer}
)

  {Residual, Result, Answer}

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satsimp
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satsimp')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satsimp.refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(x), ~Q.zero(log(x)), ~Q.zero(1 / log(x)))
Residual = -log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))

Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(x), ~Q.zero(log(x)), ~Q.zero(1 / log(x)))
Residual = -log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

sympy_extras.assumptions.simplifysympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)→-log(1/log(x)) - log(log(x))
answernull

import json as Json, re as Regex

  Quiet[Check[Message[General::argx, "W", 0], Null]];
  {$Version, Warmup, Length[GenericWarmup]}
)
(* evaluated in a forked copy of the setup kernel: TimeConstrained would not fire here (no timer thread), so the parent enforces a hard timeout of 9 s by killing this process *)
(
  Assum = (Element[x, Complexes]) && ((Re[x] == 0 && x != 0)) && ((x != 0)) && ((Log[x] != 0)) && (((1/Log[x]) != 0));
  Residual = (-Log[Log[x]]) - (Log[1/Log[x]]);
  Result = Simplify[Residual, Assum];
  Answer = Which[Result === 0, True, NumericQ[Result] && TrueQ[Result != 0], False, True, Null];
  {Residual, Result, Answer}
)

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satsimp
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satsimp')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satsimp.refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(x), ~Q.zero(log(x)), ~Q.zero(1 / log(x)))
Residual = -log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))
Result = satsimp.refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(x), ~Q.zero(log(x)), ~Q.zero(1 / log(x)))

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(x), ~Q.zero(log(x)), ~Q.zero(1 / log(x)))
Residual = -log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')
difference-log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))→-log(1/log(x)) - log(log(x))
sympy_extras.assumptions.simplifysympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)→-log(1/log(x)) - log(log(x))
answernull

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings

# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satsimp
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satsimp')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satsimp.refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(x), ~Q.zero(log(x)), ~Q.zero(1 / log(x)))
Residual = -log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))
Result = satsimp.refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')

# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)

# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): ~Q.zero(log(x))

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(x), ~Q.zero(log(x)), ~Q.zero(1 / log(x)))
Residual = -log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')

# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True, imaginary=True, zero=False)

# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): ~Q.zero(1 / log(x))

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satrefine
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satrefine')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satrefine.refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(x), ~Q.zero(log(x)), ~Q.zero(1 / log(x)))
Residual = -log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))
Result = satrefine.refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(x), ~Q.zero(log(x)), ~Q.zero(1 / log(x)))
Residual = -log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sqrt(8) + sin(-W) + exp(log(W)) - Abs(-W)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True, imaginary=True, zero=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): ~Q.zero(log(x))
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): ~Q.zero(1 / log(x))
Residual = -log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))
# automatic evaluation only: no simplify, refine or other function
Result = Residual
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satrefine
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satrefine')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satrefine.refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(x), ~Q.zero(log(x)), ~Q.zero(1 / log(x)))
Residual = -log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))
Result = satrefine.refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')
difference-log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))→-log(1/log(x)) - log(log(x))
satrefine.refinesatrefine.refine(Residual, Assum)→-log(1/log(x)) - log(log(x))
answernull

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = simplify(sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True, imaginary=True, zero=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): ~Q.zero(log(x))
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): ~Q.zero(1 / log(x))
Residual = -log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))
Result = simplify(Residual)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

simplifysimplify(Residual)→-log(1/log(x)) - log(log(x))

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(x), ~Q.zero(log(x)), ~Q.zero(1 / log(x)))
Residual = -log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = simplify(sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True, imaginary=True, zero=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): ~Q.zero(log(x))
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): ~Q.zero(1 / log(x))
Residual = -log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))
Result = simplify(Residual)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

import sympy

W = Symbol('W')

# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(x), ~Q.zero(log(x)), ~Q.zero(1 / log(x)))
Residual = -log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
(
  $HistoryLength = 0;
  $Messages = {};
  Warmup = FullSimplify[Sin[W]^2 + Cos[W]^2, Element[W, Reals]];
  GenericWarmup = Quiet[{Simplify[Abs[W] + W, W < 0], Simplify[Sqrt[W^2] - W, W > 0], Refine[Sqrt[W^2], W > 0], Simplify[Log[W V] - Log[W] - Log[V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[Log[-W] - Log[W] - I Pi, W < 0], Simplify[(-1)^(2 W) - 1, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Mod[W + 1, 2], Element[W, Integers] && Element[(W - 1)/2, Integers]], Simplify[ArcTan[W] + ArcTan[1/W] - Pi/2, W > 0], FullSimplify[ArcCot[W] - ArcTan[1/W], W > 0], Simplify[Abs[Exp[I W]] - 1, Element[W, Reals]], Simplify[Re[W] - (W + Conjugate[W])/2], Simplify[Gamma[W + 1] - W!, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0], Simplify[Floor[W] - W, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Sqrt[W] Sqrt[V] - Sqrt[W V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[HeavisideTheta[W] - 1, W > 0], Simplify[2^W 2^V - 2^(W + V), Element[W, Reals] && Element[V, Reals]], Simplify[Sum[V, {V, 1, W}] - W (W + 1)/2, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0]}];
  Quiet[Check[Message[General::argx, "W", 0], Null]];
  {$Version, Warmup, Length[GenericWarmup]}
)
(* evaluated in a forked copy of the setup kernel: TimeConstrained would not fire here (no timer thread), so the parent enforces a hard timeout of 9 s by killing this process *)
(
  Assum = (Element[x, Complexes]) && ((Re[x] == 0 && x != 0)) && ((x != 0)) && ((Log[x] != 0)) && (((1/Log[x]) != 0));
  Residual = (-Log[Log[x]]) - (Log[1/Log[x]]);
  Result = FullSimplify[Residual, Assum];
  Answer = Which[Result === 0, True, NumericQ[Result] && TrueQ[Result != 0], False, True, Null];
  {Residual, Result, Answer}
)
difference(-Log[Log[x]]) - (Log[1/Log[x]])→-Log[Log[x]^(-1)] - Log[Log[x]]
resultFullSimplify[Residual, Assum]→-Log[Log[x]^(-1)] - Log[Log[x]]

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = simplify(sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True, imaginary=True, zero=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): ~Q.zero(log(x))
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): ~Q.zero(1 / log(x))
Residual = -log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))
Result = simplify(Residual)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Q.imaginary(x), ~Q.zero(x), ~Q.zero(log(x)), ~Q.zero(1 / log(x)))
Residual = -log(log(x)) - log(1 / log(x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')
